<p><span style="font-size: 10pt;">T</span><span style="font-size: 10pt;">able 9. Antino configuration.</span></p>
<h3>Persistence</h3>
<p>Antino uses passive execution to establish persistence in the Windows Registry. It first copies itself to the hard-coded directory <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">%LOCALAPPDATA%\Windows GatherOSStateKit\tmp&lt;8 char&gt;.exe</span> and attempts to copy two DLLs, <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">DllSafeCheck64.dll</span> and <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">slc.dll</span>, from its current directory.</p>
<p>Antino does not import either of these files, but we have observed it using <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">DllSafeCheck64.dll</span> for sideloading in older versions of Antino. This functionality is likely a remnant from those versions.</p>
<p>The implant then checks for the presence of a file named <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">id</span>. If it exists, Antino reads the contents as the bot ID. If the file does not exist, it generates a UUID string and writes it to the <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">id</span> file.<em> </em></p>
<p>To achieve passive execution, the implant writes a diagnostic answer file to <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">%TEMP%\SDIAG_&lt;GUID&gt;\result\result.ps1</span> as shown below in Figure 28. A diagnostic answer file is a configuration file that defines parameters for the diagnosed application.</p>
<figure id="attachment_189607" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-189607" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img  class="wp-image-189607 lozad"  data-src="https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-155708-189298-28.png" alt="A snippet of Windows PowerShell script is displayed. It defines variables to gather system diagnostics using an executable. The script contains paths for file and registry item creation." width="1000" height="262" data-srcset="https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-155708-189298-28.png 2048w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-155708-189298-28-786x206.png 786w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-155708-189298-28-1920x503.png 1920w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-155708-189298-28-768x201.png 768w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-155708-189298-28-1536x402.png 1536w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-189607" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 28. Diagnostic answer file contents.</figcaption></figure>
<p>Antino executes the Microsoft Scripted Diagnostic Native Host utility (<span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">sdiagnhost.exe</span>), which parses the diagnostic answer file. The first XML section highlighted in red defines the header, and the text that follows is the path required to load the Program Compatibility Wizard (PCW). The contents highlighted in purple are the answer file folder name. The contents highlighted in blue are the answer file filename. The PowerShell snippet highlighted in light magenta is ultimately executed in the context of <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">sdiagnhost.exe</span> and adds Antino to the Windows Registry Run key.</p>
<h3>Command and Control</h3>
<p>Antino uses Microsoft OneDrive for C2 and uploads a heartbeat to the <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">antino/heartbeat/&lt;Bot ID&gt;.json</span> file in the OneDrive folder as a beacon. The heartbeat contains the hostname, username and remark from the Antino configuration.</p>
<p>Antino searches the OneDrive root folder for files following the <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">command_req_&lt;Bot ID&gt;_&lt;timestamp&gt;.json</span> format. Files matching the format indicate that a command has been issued to the bot. Figure 29 below shows an example command, <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">system_info</span>.</p>
<figure id="attachment_189618" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-189618" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img  class="wp-image-189618 lozad"  data-src="https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-158343-189298-29.png" alt="Screenshot of a JSON code snippet displaying system information with command type and subject ID." width="1000" height="194" data-srcset="https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-158343-189298-29.png 1880w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-158343-189298-29-786x152.png 786w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-158343-189298-29-768x149.png 768w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-158343-189298-29-1536x297.png 1536w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-189618" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 29. Antino system_info command example.</figcaption></figure>
<figure id="attachment_189629" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-189629" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img  class="wp-image-189629 lozad"  data-src="https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-160698-189298-30.png" alt="A JSON formatted system information output displaying details such as command type, system info, machine name, platform as &quot;windows&quot;, with remarks redacted. It also includes session and process IDs, followed by success status as true." width="1000" height="374" data-srcset="https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-160698-189298-30.png 2048w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-160698-189298-30-786x294.png 786w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-160698-189298-30-1872x700.png 1872w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-160698-189298-30-768x287.png 768w, https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/word-image-160698-189298-30-1536x575.png 1536w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-189629" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 30. Antino <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">system_info</span> command response example.</figcaption></figure>
<p>Antino supports 10 commands shown below in Table 10.</p>
<table style="width: 99.9041%;">
<tbody>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%; text-align: center;"><strong>Command</strong></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%; text-align: center;"><strong>Description</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">cmd</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">Shell command execution</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">ps</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">PowerShell execution</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">system_info</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">System information collection</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">list_files</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">Directory listing</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">add_to_run</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">Registry Run key persistence</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">exit</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">Client shutdown</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">load</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">Download and execute a Rhai script</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">execute_program</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">Program execution</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">upload_file</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">File exfiltration via OneDrive, uploaded to <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">antino/antino_upload/</span></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="width: 19.6947%;"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">download_file</span></td>
<td style="width: 170.687%;">File download from OneDrive, downloaded from <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">antino/antino_upload/</span></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p><span style="font-size: 10pt;">Table 10. Antino commands.</span></p>
<p>Notably, Antino supports execution of Rhai scripts (embedded scripting for Rust) that easily extend the implants’ capabilities without compiling additional binary shellcode. We have not observed any usage of Rhai scripts in the wild.</p>
<h2>Conclusion</h2>
<p>Falcon's browser-native approach presents significant challenges for traditional endpoint and network security controls. It operates entirely within the browser sandbox, avoiding persistent file artifacts and encrypting all C2 traffic over WebSocket.</p>
<p>The discovery of Antino, a Rust-based implant that routes its C2 through Microsoft OneDrive, demonstrates further attempts to blend into network traffic. Organizations should monitor for the following anomalous activity:</p>
<ul>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1">Browser extensions installed in developer mode, particularly those mimicking common utilities (e.g., PDF viewers) and requesting broad permissions (e.g., <span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">&lt;all_urls&gt;, debugger, tabCapture, nativeMessaging, <span style="font-family: georgia, palatino, serif;">or </span></span><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">webRequest</span>)</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">Hm_lvt_</span> cookies or local-storage keys on internal or government web applications where Baidu Analytics has no legitimate reason to appear</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">&lt;script&gt;</span> tags added to the templates or headers of internal webmail, ticketing or case-management portals loading resources from unfamiliar external domains, especially where the response is a heavily numeric JavaScript array with a small decoder loop</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1">OAuth activity to Microsoft OneDrive tenants outside the organization's own tenancy, especially with repeated small JSON writes to paths such as antino<span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;">/heartbeat/</span><span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier, monospace;"> or antino/antino_upload/</span></li>
</ul>
<h3>Palo Alto Networks Protection and Mitigation</h3>
<p>Palo Alto Networks customers are better protected from the threats discussed above through the following products:</p>
<ul>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1">The <a href="https://docs.paloaltonetworks.com/wildfire" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Advanced WildFire</a> machine-learning models and analysis techniques have been reviewed and updated in light of the indicators shared in this research.</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><a href="https://docs.paloaltonetworks.com/advanced-url-filtering/administration/url-filtering-basics/how-url-filtering-works" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Advanced URL Filtering</a> and <a href="https://docs.paloaltonetworks.com/dns-security/administration/about-dns-security" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Advanced DNS Security</a> identify known URLs and domains associated with this activity as malicious.</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><a href="https://docs-cortex.paloaltonetworks.com/p/XDR" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cortex XDR</a> and <a href="https://docs-cortex.paloaltonetworks.com/p/XSIAM" target="_blank" rel="noopener">XSIAM</a> are designed to prevent the execution of known malicious malware and prevent the execution of unknown malware using Behavioral Threat Protection and machine learning based on the Local Analysis module.</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><a href="https://www.koi.ai/product/endpoint" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cortex Agentic Endpoint Security</a> is designed to help discover every AI artifact and AI agent's activity across the agentic endpoint, assess its risk, enforce prevention and runtime controls and remediate violations.</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><a href="https://docs.paloaltonetworks.com/prisma-access-browser" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Prisma Browser</a> is designed to provide multi-layered protection against extension-based threats by preventing the side-loading of unauthorized extensions, as well as detecting, blocking, and removing malicious extensions in the event of manual user installation.’</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><a href="https://docs.cyberark.com/epm/latest/en/content/intro/introduction.htm" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Idira Endpoint Privilege Manager (EPM)</a> can help enable enterprises to reduce risk, satisfy compliance and streamline operations. It helps implement least privilege via policy-driven elevation and removal of standing admin rights, and blocks risky actions such as executing unvetted applications and access to memory of other processes, while providing audit-ready evidence and unified identity governance.</li>
<li style="font-weight: 400;" aria-level="1"><a href="https://www.paloaltonetworks.com/idira/human/identity-and-access-management" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Idira Identity and Access Management (IAM)</a> can help enable secure, unified access across human, machine and AI identities, balancing granular control with a seamless user experience. Powered by SSO, phishing-resistant MFA, passwordless login and enterprise password management, Idira IAM extends intelligent privilege controls and session security across the broader workforce.</li>
</ul>
<p>Organizations can access tailored adversary intelligence, direct analyst consultations, and specialized exposure tracking through <a href="https://www.paloaltonetworks.com/unit42/threat-intelligence" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Unit 42 Threat Intel Services</a> and its <a href="https://www.paloaltonetworks.com/resources/datasheets/unit-42-deep-and-dark-web-service" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deep and Dark Web Service</a>.</p>
<p>If you think you may have been compromised or have an urgent matter, get in touch with the <a href="https://start.paloaltonetworks.com/contact-unit42.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Unit 42 Incident Response team</a> or call:</p>

<script>
 var PAN_LOAD_RECAPTCHA_LANGUAGE = 'en_US';

(function($) {
	// Migrated from the unit42-v5 + Modifications
	var subscribeSuccess = false;
	var email = document.getElementById('newsletter-email');
	var subscription_form = document.getElementById('unit42footerSubscription_form');
	var subscription_form_button = document.getElementById('unit42footerSubscription_form_button');
        var isCaptchaComplete02  = false;	
        window.captchaInlineFormOnload = function(token){
            isCaptchaComplete02 = true;
            $("form[name='Unit42_Subscribe'] button[id='unit42footerSubscription_form_button']").prop('disabled', false);
        }
        var PAN_INLINE_FORM_RECAPTCHA_LOADED = false;
        const SITE_KEY = '6LdDKhIsAAAAABdoYV3PR5VJ2Gz23_dLSvq2uvBQ'; 
                if(!PAN_INLINE_FORM_RECAPTCHA_LOADED){
               PAN_INLINE_FORM_RECAPTCHA_LOADED = true;
               // trigger loading api.js (recaptcha.js) script
               var recapthaScript = $("script[src='https://www.google.com/recaptcha/enterprise.js?render="+SITE_KEY+"&hl="+PAN_LOAD_RECAPTCHA_LANGUAGE+"&onload=captchaInlineFormOnload']").length
               // console.log("recapthaScript",recapthaScript);
               if (!recapthaScript) {
                   var head = document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
                   var script = document.createElement('script');
                   script.typerecapthaScript = 'text/javascript';
                   script.src = "https://www.google.com/recaptcha/enterprise.js?render="+SITE_KEY+"&hl="+PAN_LOAD_RECAPTCHA_LANGUAGE+"&onload=captchaInlineFormOnload";
                   head.appendChild(script);
               }
               else{
                   captchaInlineFormOnload();
                }
           }
            	
	
	$(subscription_form).submit(function(e) {
		e.preventDefault();
		e.stopImmediatePropagation();
		updateEmailMask();
		var success = true;
		var form = $(this);
		var mail = form.find('input[name="Email"]');
		
		if (mail.val() === '') {
			mail.addClass('has-error');
			showError(1);
			success = false;	
		}  else if (!isEmail(mail.val())){
			showError(2);
			success = false;
		}
		else {
			mail.removeClass('has-error');
			$('.error-mail').addClass('d-none');
		}

		

		if (success) {
                                                if(grecaptcha){
                        grecaptcha.enterprise.ready(function() {
                            grecaptcha.enterprise.execute(SITE_KEY, {action: 'submit'}).then(function(token) {
                                submitSubscribeForm({token :token, action : 'submit'},form);
                            });
                        });
                    }
                    			
		}
		return false;
	});
        function submitSubscribeForm(tokenData, form){
            var serialized_data = form.serialize();
            let captchaParams = `&g-recaptcha-response=${tokenData.token}&action=${tokenData.action}`;
            $.ajax({
				type: 'POST',
				url: form.attr('action'),
				data: serialized_data+captchaParams,
				beforeSend: function() {
                 form.find('button').addClass('is-loading');
    			},
				success: function(msg) {
					form.find('.form-success-message').html('<p class="success-message">You have been successfully subscribed</p>');
					form.find('button').removeClass('is-loading');
					$(email).val('');
					clearError();
				},
				error: function(jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
					$(subscription_form_button).addClass('is-disabled', true);
					form.find('button').removeClass('is-loading');
					
				}
			});
            
        }
	function showError(error_type){
		if(error_type == 1) {
			$('.error-mail').text("Please enter the email address.").addClass('error-show');
			$(subscription_form_button).addClass('is-disabled');
		} else if(error_type == 2){
			$('.error-mail').text("Please provide a valid e-mail address.").addClass('error-show');
			$(subscription_form_button).addClass('is-disabled');
		}	
		$(subscription_form_button).removeClass('is-loading');
	}
	function clearError(){
		$('.error-mail').text("").removeClass('error-show');;
		$(subscription_form_button).removeClass('is-loading');
		$(subscription_form_button).removeClass('is-disabled');
	}
	
	$(email).on('input', function (event) { 
		var email = $(this).val();
		if (isEmail(email) ) {			
			clearError();
		} else if(email == ""){
			clearError();
		} else{
			showError(2);			  
		}
    });

	function isEmail(email) {
		var re = /^(([^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+(\.[^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+)*)|(".+"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
		return re.test(String(email).toLowerCase());
	}
	
	
	
	function updateEmailMask() {
		var email = $("#unit42footerSubscription_form input[name='Email']").val();
		if (email && email.trim() != '') {
			var maskedEmail = maskEmailAddress(email);
			$("#unit42footerSubscription_form input[name='emailFormMask']").val(maskedEmail);
		}
	}

	function maskEmailAddress (emailAddress) {
		function mask(str) {
			var strLen = str.length;
			if (strLen > 4) {
				return str.substr(0, 1) + str.substr(1, strLen - 1).replace(/\w/g, '*') + str.substr(-1,1);
			}
			return str.replace(/\w/g, '*');
		}
		return emailAddress.replace(/([\w.]+)@([\w.]+)(\.[\w.]+)/g, function (m, p1, p2, p3) {
			return mask(p1) + '@' + mask(p2) + p3;
		});

		return emailAddress;
	}

}(jQuery));
//# sourceMappingURL=main.js.map
	
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
	$(document).ready(function(){
		$("html").on("click", ".language-btn", function () {
			$(this).toggleClass("expand");
			$(".language-dropdown").toggleClass("expand");
		});

		$("html").on("click", "body", function (b) {
			if (!$(b.target).is(".language-btn")) {
				$(".language-btn").removeClass("expand");
				$(".language-dropdown").removeClass("expand");
			}
		});
	});
</script>

	var isProcessing = false; 
    function alter_ul_post_values(obj,post_id,ul_type){
	
		if (isProcessing)    
		return;  
		isProcessing = true;   
		var like_nonce = jQuery('#_wpnonce').val();
		jQuery(obj).find("span").html("..");
                jQuery.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    url: "https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/wp-content/plugins/like-dislike-counter-for-posts-pages-and-comments/ajax_counter.php",
                    data: "post_id="+post_id+"&up_type="+ul_type+"&ul_nonce="+like_nonce,
                    success: function(msg){
                            jQuery(obj).find("span").html(msg);
                            isProcessing = false; 
                            jQuery(obj).find('svg').children('path').attr('stroke','#0050FF');
                            jQuery(obj).removeClass('idc_ul_cont_not_liked idc_ul_cont_not_liked_inner');
                    }
 		});
	}

}

function loadScript(url, defer){
    var script1 = document.createElement('script');
    script1.setAttribute('type', 'text/javascript');
    script1.setAttribute('src',url);
    if(defer == true){
        script1.setAttribute('defer','defer');
    }
    document.head.appendChild(script1);
}
function loadScript1(url, callback){

    var script = document.createElement("script")
    script.type = "text/javascript";
    if (script.readyState){  //IE
        script.onreadystatechange = function(){
            if (script.readyState == "loaded" || script.readyState == "complete"){
                script.onreadystatechange = null;
                callback();
            }
        };
    } else {  //Others
        script.onload = function(){
            callback();
        };
    }
    script.src = url;
    document.getElementsByTagName("head")[0].appendChild(script);
}

    setTimeout(function(){
      
      loadScript1(main_site_critical_top2, function(){
        window.PAN_initialize2021Nav();
        loadScript(main_site_defered2, false);
      });      
            loadScript(loadSubNav, false);
          }, 3000);

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  // Find all the forms on the page
  var forms = document.getElementsByTagName("form");

  // Loop through each form
  for (var i = 0; i < forms.length; i++) {
    var form = forms[i];

    // Check if the hidden input field already exists
    var requestInput = form.querySelector("input[name='web_unit42_req_id']");
    if (!requestInput) {
      // Create a hidden input element if it doesn't exist
      requestInput = document.createElement("input");
      requestInput.type = "hidden";
      requestInput.name = "web_unit42_req_id";
      requestInput.id = "web_unit42_req_id";

      // Append the hidden field to the form
      form.appendChild(requestInput);
    }
  }
});

// Assign the onCheqResponse function to the window object to handle events from any form
window.onCheqResponse = function(encryptedMessage, requestID) {
  // Find all the forms on the page
  var forms = document.getElementsByTagName("form");

  for (var i = 0; i < forms.length; i++) {
    var form = forms[i];
    var requestInput = form.querySelector("input[name='web_unit42_req_id']");
    if (requestInput) {
      requestInput.value = requestID;
    }
  }
};

var languageFromPage = 'en_US';
(function () {
  console.log("IIFE Loaded: Global Form Pixel Tracking Script");

  // Target countries matching requirements
  var TARGET_COUNTRIES = ['FR', 'IT'];
  window.GEO_LOCATION_FR = false;

  // Mapping full country select option values/texts to 2-letter codes or names
  function isTargetCountryString(str) {
    if (!str) return false;
    var normalized = str.trim().toUpperCase();
    return (
      normalized === 'FR' ||
      normalized === 'FRANCE' ||
      normalized === 'IT' ||
      normalized === 'ITALY'
    );
  }

  // Helper to match target country names
  function isTargetCountryName(country) {
    if (!country) return false;
    var normalized = country.trim().toUpperCase();
    return (
      normalized === 'FRANCE' ||
      normalized === 'ITALY' ||
      normalized === 'FR' ||
      normalized === 'IT'
    );
  }

  // Get country name dynamically based on OneTrust country code
  function getGeoLocationCountryName() {
    if (typeof OneTrust !== 'undefined' && typeof OneTrust.getGeolocationData === 'function') {
      var geoData = OneTrust.getGeolocationData();
      if (geoData && geoData.country) {
        var code = geoData.country.toUpperCase();
        if (code === 'IT') return 'Italy';
        if (code === 'FR') return 'France';
      }
    }
    return '';
  }

  function getLocalizedValue(locale) {
    switch (locale) {
      case 'en_US':
        return "Allow email engagement tracking (opens and clicks) for personalized content.";
      case 'zh_CN':
        return "允许跟踪电子邮件互动情况（打开和点击），以便提供个性化内容。";
      case 'zh_TW':
        return "允許電子郵件互動追蹤 (開啟與點擊) 以提供個人化內容。";
      case 'es_ES':
        return "Permitir el seguimiento de mis interacciones con los correos electrónicos (aperturas y clics) para recibir contenidos personalizados.";
      case 'es_LA':
        return "Permitir el seguimiento de la interacción con los correos electrónicos (aperturas y clics) para ofrecer contenido personalizado.";
      case 'pt_BR':
        return "Permitir o rastreamento do engajamento por e-mail (aberturas e cliques) para conteúdo personalizado.";
      case 'ko_KR':
        return "개인화된 콘텐츠의 이메일 참여 추적(열기 및 클릭)을 허용합니다.";
      case 'fr_FR':
        return "J’autorise le suivi de mes interactions avec les e-mails (ouvertures et clics) afin de recevoir des contenus personnalisés.";
      case 'de_DE':
        return "Gestatten Sie die Erfassung von E-Mail-Interaktionen (Öffnungen und Klicks), um personalisierte Inhalte zu erhalten.";
      case 'it_IT':
        return "Consenti il monitoraggio delle interazioni via e-mail (aperture e clic) per contenuti personalizzati.";
      case 'ja_JP':
        return "コンテンツのパーソナライズのため、メール エンゲージメントの追跡(開封とクリック)を許可。";
      default:
        return "Allow email engagement tracking (opens and clicks) for personalized content.";
    }
  }

  // Evaluate initial GeoLocation state from OneTrust
  function isTargetGeoLocation() {
    if (typeof OneTrust !== 'undefined' && typeof OneTrust.getGeolocationData === 'function') {
      var geoData = OneTrust.getGeolocationData();

      console.log("OneTrust GeoLocation Data:", geoData, "Country:", geoData ? geoData.country : 'N/A');

      if (geoData && geoData.country) {
        var country = geoData.country.toUpperCase();
        if (TARGET_COUNTRIES.indexOf(country) !== -1) {
          window.GEO_LOCATION_FR = true;
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    window.GEO_LOCATION_FR = false;
    return false;
  }

  // Create the standard checkbox DOM structure based on form theme
  function createCheckboxElement(form) {
    var wrapper;

    // Check if form uses the custom theme format (e.g., Form 3, 4, 5)
    var isCustomTheme = form.querySelector('.captcha-section') || form.querySelector('.checkbox');

    if (isCustomTheme && form.id != 'pan_prisma_microsite_modal_form') {
      wrapper = document.createElement('div');
      wrapper.className = 'form-row email-pixel-wrapper mt-2 mb-2';

      var labelWrapper = document.createElement('label');
      labelWrapper.className = 'checkbox';

      if($(form).hasClass("form_events_vutdProgramme")){
        labelWrapper.className += " subscribe";
      }
      var spanIcon = document.createElement('span');
      spanIcon.className = 'checkbox-icon checkbox';

      var checkbox = document.createElement('input');
      checkbox.type = 'checkbox';
      checkbox.name = 'emailPixelTracking';
      checkbox.id = 'emailPixelTracking_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
      checkbox.value = 'true';

      var iconInner = document.createElement('span');
      iconInner.className = 'icon d-flex';

      spanIcon.appendChild(checkbox);
      spanIcon.appendChild(iconInner);

      var spanText = document.createElement('span');
      var textClass;
      // Detect light or dark mode styling
      if(form.querySelector('.text-dark') == null){
        textClass = 'label-2 legal display-1';
      }else{
        textClass = form.querySelector('.text-dark') ? 'label-2 text-dark' : 'label-2 text-light';
      }
      if(!$(form).hasClass("form_events_vutdProgramme")){
        spanText.className = textClass;
      }
      spanText.textContent = getLocalizedValue(languageFromPage);

      labelWrapper.appendChild(spanIcon);
      labelWrapper.appendChild(spanText);
      wrapper.appendChild(labelWrapper);
    } else {
      // Standard Bootstrap format (e.g., Form 1, 2)
      wrapper = document.createElement('div');
      wrapper.className = 'form-check checkbox-wrapper mb-2 d-flex align-items-center email-pixel-wrapper';
      if(form.name == "create_user_form" && !$(form).find(".checkbox-container").length){
        wrapper.style = "gap:10px";
      }
      var checkbox = document.createElement('input');
      checkbox.type = 'checkbox';
      checkbox.name = 'emailPixelTracking';
      checkbox.id = 'emailPixelTracking_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
      checkbox.className = 'form-check-input mb-0';
      checkbox.value = 'true';
      checkbox.style = "width:auto;";
      let $sticyform = $(".sticky-form-html");
      if($sticyform.length){
        $sticyform.find(".checkbox-wrapper").find("input").css("flex","0");
        $sticyform.find(".checkbox-wrapper").find("span").addClass("ml-2");
        checkbox.style += "flex:0;margin-top:0px;";
      }
      var label = document.createElement('span');
      label.htmlFor = checkbox.id;

      // Handle text color based on form layout
      var isWhiteText = form.querySelector('.text-white');
      if(form.name == "bootcamp-registration"){
        wrapper.className = wrapper.className + " col-12 mb-4";
      }
      label.className = 'form-check-label label-3  ' +  (isWhiteText ? 'text-white ' : '') + ' ml-2 mb-0 pb-0';
      label.textContent = getLocalizedValue(languageFromPage);
      if(form.id == "footer_form_manage_subscriptions"){
         label.style = "margin-left:0.75rem";
      }
      wrapper.appendChild(checkbox);
      wrapper.appendChild(label);
    }

    return wrapper;
  }

  var buildDropdown = function(result, dropdown){
    // Remove current options
    dropdown.html('');

    // Check result isn't empty
    if(result && result.length > 0){
        // Loop through each of the results and append the option to the dropdown
        $.each(result, function() {
            dropdown.append('<option value="' + this.value + '">' + this.label + '</option>');
        });
    }
  };

  function dropdownInit(){
    if(window.countryOptions && isTargetGeoLocation()){
      let $country = $('select[name="Country"]');
      if(!$country.length){
        $country = $('select[name="country"]');
      }
      $.each($country, function(index, element) {
        buildDropdown(window.countryOptions, $(element));
      });
    }
  }

  $(document).on('click', '#notyou, [data-cmp-hook="notyou"]', function (e) {
    const $form = $(this).closest('form');

    setTimeout(function () {
      let $countryInput = $form.find('[name="Country"]');
      if(!$countryInput.length){
        $countryInput = $form.find('[name="country"]');
      }
      if(isTargetGeoLocation()){
        injectCheckbox($form.get(0));
      }else{
        removeCheckbox($form.get(0));
        $countryInput.val('');
      }
    }, 110);
  });

  const originalFetch = window.fetch;

  // Override window.fetch
  window.fetch = async function (...args) {
    let [resource, config] = args;

    try {
      const response = await originalFetch.apply(this, [resource, config]);
      if(resource == "/apps/pan/public/filterleads.getLeadDetailsForWebDataLayer.json"){
        const response2 = response.clone();
        const data = await response2.json();
        if (data.success && data.data.Email) {
          var forms = document.querySelectorAll('form');
          forms.forEach(function (form) {
            if(isTargetCountryName(data.data.Country)){
              $(form).find('[name="Country"]').val(data.data.Country);
              injectCheckbox(form);
            }
            else{
              removeCheckbox(form);
            }
          });
        }
      }

      return response;
    } catch (error) {
      throw error;
    }
  };

  $(document).ajaxComplete(function(event, xhr, settings) {
    if (settings.url.includes('/etc/formsconfig/webformsconfig.json') ) {
      if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
          window.countryOptions = xhr.responseJSON.country[languageFromPage || PAN_LOAD_RECAPTCHA_LANGUAGE];
          dropdownInit();
      }
    }
    if (settings.url.includes('/apps/pan/public/filterleads.getLeadDetailsForWebDataLayer.json') ) {
      if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
        if(xhr.responseJSON.success && xhr.responseJSON.data.Email){
          var forms = document.querySelectorAll('form');
          forms.forEach(function (form) {
            if(isTargetCountryName(xhr.responseJSON.data.Country)){
              $(form).find('[name="Country"]').val(xhr.responseJSON.data.Country);
              injectCheckbox(form);
            }
            else{
              removeCheckbox(form);
            }
          });
        }
      }
    }
  });

  $(document).ajaxSend(function(event, jqXHR, settings) {
    if (settings.url.includes('/apps/pan/public/formsubmithandler.submitform.json')) {
      let params = new URLSearchParams(settings.data || '');
      if($('[name="emailPixelTracking"]').length){
        if(params.get("emailPixelTracking") == 'true'){
          params.set('emailPixelTracking', true);
        }
        else{
          params.set('emailPixelTracking', false);
        }
      }
      settings.data = params.toString();
    }
  });

  // Dynamically inject the checkbox inside a form based on structure
  function injectCheckbox(form) {
    if (form.querySelector('input[name="emailPixelTracking"]')) return;

    var checkboxEl = createCheckboxElement(form);

    var captchaSection = form.querySelector('.captcha-section');
    if (captchaSection) {
      var legalInCaptcha = captchaSection.querySelector('.legal');
      if (legalInCaptcha) {
        captchaSection.insertBefore(checkboxEl, legalInCaptcha);
        return;
      }
      captchaSection.insertBefore(checkboxEl, captchaSection.firstChild);
      return;
    }

    var legalContainer = form.querySelectorAll('.form-legal');
    if (legalContainer.length) {
      legalContainer = legalContainer[0];
      let legalparentElement = legalContainer.parentElement;
      if(legalparentElement.classList.contains('form-row')){
        legalparentElement.parentElement.insertBefore(checkboxEl, legalparentElement);
      }
      else{
         legalContainer.insertBefore(checkboxEl, legalContainer.firstChild);
      }
      return;
    }

    var legalContainer = form.querySelectorAll('.legal');
    if (legalContainer.length) {
      legalContainer = legalContainer.length > 2 ? legalContainer[1] : legalContainer[0];
      let legalparentElement = legalContainer.parentElement;
      if(!legalparentElement.classList.contains('terms')){
        if(legalparentElement.classList.contains('form-row')){
          legalparentElement.parentElement.insertBefore(checkboxEl, legalparentElement);
        }
        else{
          legalContainer.insertBefore(checkboxEl, legalContainer.firstChild);
        }
        return;
      }
    }

    var termsSubmit = form.querySelector('.terms-submit');
    if (termsSubmit) {
      termsSubmit.insertBefore(checkboxEl, termsSubmit.firstChild);
      return;
    }

    var terms = form.querySelector('.terms') || form.querySelector('.terms-condition');
    if (terms) {
      if($(terms).hasClass("checkbox-wrapper")){
        terms = $(terms).next().length ?  $(terms).next().get(0) : terms;
      }
      terms.before(checkboxEl);
      return;
    }
    var terms = form.querySelector('.checkbox-container');
    if (terms) {
      $(terms).append(checkboxEl).find(".label-3").removeClass("label-3");
      return;
    }
  }

  // Remove checkbox if country selection changes away from targeted countries
  function removeCheckbox(form) {
    var existingWrapper = form.querySelector('.email-pixel-wrapper');
    if (existingWrapper) {
      existingWrapper.parentNode.removeChild(existingWrapper);
    }
  }

  // Evaluate dynamic state for a specific form
  function evaluateForm(form, isGeoMatch) {
    if (isGeoMatch) {
      injectCheckbox(form);
      var detectedCountry = getGeoLocationCountryName();
      if (detectedCountry && !$(form).find('[name="Country"]').length && !$(form).find('[name="country"]').length) {
        $(form).prepend('<input type="hidden" name="Country" id="Country" value="' + detectedCountry + '"></input>');
      }
      return;
    }

    var countrySelect = form.querySelector('select[name="Country"]') || form.querySelector('select[name="country"]');
    if (countrySelect) {
      var selectedValue = countrySelect.value;
      var selectedText = countrySelect.options[countrySelect.selectedIndex]
        ? countrySelect.options[countrySelect.selectedIndex].text
        : '';

      if (isTargetCountryString(selectedValue) || isTargetCountryString(selectedText)) {
        injectCheckbox(form);
      } else {
        removeCheckbox(form);
      }
    }
  }

  // Attach change listener to country dropdowns
  function bindCountrySelectListeners(isGeoMatch) {
    var countrySelects = document.querySelectorAll('select[name="Country"]');
    if(!countrySelects.length){
      countrySelects = document.querySelectorAll('select[name="country"]');
    }
    countrySelects.forEach(function (select) {
      if (select.dataset.pixelListenerAttached) return;

      select.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
        var form = select.closest('form');
        if (form && !isGeoMatch) {
          evaluateForm(form, isGeoMatch);
        } else if(!isTargetCountryName(e.target.value) && isGeoMatch) {
          removeCheckbox(form);
        } else if(isTargetCountryName(e.target.value)) {
          injectCheckbox(form);
        }
      });
      select.dataset.pixelListenerAttached = "true";
    });
  }

  // Main evaluation function across all forms
  function processAllForms() {
    var isGeoMatch = isTargetGeoLocation();
    var forms = document.querySelectorAll('form');
    if(isGeoMatch){
      dropdownInit();
    }

    forms.forEach(function (form) {
      evaluateForm(form, isGeoMatch);
    });

    bindCountrySelectListeners(isGeoMatch);
  }

  // --- Integrations & Hooks ---

  // 1. OneTrust OptanonWrapper Hook
  var originalOptanonWrapper = window.OptanonWrapper;
  window.OptanonWrapper = function () {
    if (typeof originalOptanonWrapper === 'function') {
      originalOptanonWrapper();
    }
    processAllForms();
  };

  // 2. OneTrust Live Consent Updates Listener
  window.addEventListener('OneTrustGroupsUpdated', function () {
    processAllForms();
  });

  // 3. Document Ready Execution
  if (document.readyState === 'interactive' || document.readyState === 'complete') {
    processAllForms();
  } else {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', processAllForms);
  }
  processAllForms();
})();
</script>
